Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any superconductors module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 191. The projective resolution has length at most 799 with Betti numbers bounded by 3.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 191 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the superconductors structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 799. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
